"1989","We are supposed to tip at least fifteen percent.","Ngo5 dei6 jing1 goi1 jiu3 bei2 zoi3 siu2 sap6 ng5 sing1 ge3 siu2 fei3.","我哋應該要畀至少十五成嘅小費。"
"1990","Someone mentioned your name at the party.","Hai2 go3 paai3 deoi3 dou6 jau5 jan4 tai4 gwo3 nei5 go3 meng2.","喺個派對度有人提過你個名。"
"1991","We take turns listening to each other.","Ngo5 dei6 leon4 cau4 ting1 deoi3 fong1 gong2 je5.","我哋輪流聽對方講嘢。"
"1992","The truth had not been revealed yet.","Zan1 soeng1 zung6 mei6 bei6 git3 lou6.","真相仲未被揭露。"
"1993","I wish I could turn back time.","Ngo5 hei1 mong6 ngo5 ho2 ji5 zoeng1 si4 gaan1 zyun2 faan1 zyun2 tau4.","我希望我可以將時間轉返轉頭。"
"1994","We are supposed to forgive and forget.","Ngo5 dei6 jing1 goi1 jyun4 loeng4 tung4 mong6 gei3.","我哋應該原諒同忘記。"
"1995","Someone left a great review.","Jau5 jan4 lau4 zo2 go3 hou2 zing3 ge3 ping4 leon6.","有人留咗個好正嘅評論。"
"1996","We take turns choosing the movie.","Ngo5 dei6 leon4 cau4 gaai1 tou6 hei3.","我哋輪流揀套戲。"
"1997","The venue had not been confirmed yet.","Coeng4 dei6 zung6 mei6 kwan4 jan4.","場地仲未確認。"
